def storage:
  stor0 is uint8 at storage 0
  owner is address at storage 0 offset 8
  exchangeTokenAddress is address at storage 1
  registryAddress is address at storage 2
  tokenTransferProxyAddress is address at storage 3
  stor4 is mapping of uint8 at storage 4
  stor5 is mapping of uint256 at storage 5
  nonces is mapping of uint256 at storage 6
  minimumMakerProtocolFee is uint256 at storage 7
  minimumTakerProtocolFee is uint256 at storage 8
  protocolFeeRecipientAddress is address at storage 9

def renounceOwnership(): # not payable
  require caller == owner
  log OwnershipRenounced(address previousOwner=owner)
  owner = 0

  protocolFeeRecipientAddress = newProtocolFeeRecipient

def changeMinimumTakerProtocolFee(uint256 newMinimumTakerProtocolFee): # not payable
  require caller == owner
  minimumTakerProtocolFee = newMinimumTakerProtocolFee

def incrementNonce(): # not payable
  nonces[caller]++
  log NonceIncremented(
        address maker=(nonces[caller] + 1),
        uint256 newNonce=caller)

def transferOwnership(address newOwner): # not payable
  require caller == owner
  require newOwner
  log OwnershipTransferred(
        address previousOwner=owner,
        address newOwner=newOwner)
  owner = newOwner
